Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The energy variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 561 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 39 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 561 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the energy structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 39. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
